=== FILE: schemas/dossier.py ===
"""Authoritative Dossier and Correlation Schemas.

Conforms to contracts/EVIDENCE_SCHEMA.md and contracts/CORRELATION_ENGINE.md.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime
from enum import Enum
from typing import Any, Optional
from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field
from .evidence import EvidenceRecord


class DossierStatus(str, Enum):
    OK = "ok"
    PARTIAL = "partial"
    ERROR = "error"


class RelationshipResult(str, Enum):
    CONFIRMED = "confirmed"
    CANDIDATE = "candidate"
    AMBIGUOUS = "ambiguous"
    REJECTED = "rejected"


class RelationshipRecord(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="ignore")

    relationship_id: str = Field(..., description="Unique relationship identifier (rel-*)")
    type: str = Field(..., description="Semantic link type e.g. pod_covered_by_pdb, pod_bound_to_pvc")
    from_evidence_id: str = Field(..., description="Source evidence ID")
    to_evidence_id: str = Field(..., description="Target evidence ID")
    join_rule: str = Field(..., description="Deterministic join rule identifier")
    result: RelationshipResult = Field(..., description="Evaluation outcome of the relationship")
    reason: str = Field(..., description="Explanation of match or mismatch rationale")


class AdapterQueryResult(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="ignore")

    adapter_name: str = Field(..., description="Name of the adapter (splunk_mcp, prometheus_mcp)")
    status: DossierStatus = Field(..., description="Query execution status: ok, partial, error")
    execution_time_ms: int = Field(0, description="Latency of the query call")
    records_count: int = Field(0, description="Number of telemetry records returned")
    error_code: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Standardized error code if failed")
    error_message: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Redacted error description if failed")


class CollectionWindow(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="ignore")

    earliest: datetime = Field(..., description="Query window earliest boundary")
    latest: datetime = Field(..., description="Query window latest boundary")


class Dossier(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="ignore")

    investigation_id: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Correlated investigation ID")
    cluster_id: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Target cluster ID")
    node_name: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Target node name")
    status: DossierStatus = Field(DossierStatus.OK, description="Dossier aggregate status: ok, partial, error")
    observed_at: datetime = Field(..., description="Timestamp of dossier observation/collection")
    execution_time_ms: int = Field(0, description="Total elapsed evidence gathering time")
    collection_window: Optional[CollectionWindow] = Field(None, description="Time range queried")
    adapter_results: list[AdapterQueryResult] = Field(default_factory=list, description="Per-adapter call details")
    records: list[dict[str, Any]] = Field(default_factory=list, description="Correlated incident records")
    evidence_items: list[EvidenceRecord] = Field(default_factory=list, description="Normalized canonical evidence items")
    relationships: list[RelationshipRecord] = Field(default_factory=list, description="Deterministic entity relationships")
    missing_evidence: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list, description="Declared evidence requirements that were absent")
    contradictions: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list, description="Contradictory evidence signals detected")
    freshness: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Aggregate freshness marker (fresh, stale, partial)")

=== FILE: schemas/event.py ===
"""Authoritative Event Envelope and Ingress Contract Schemas.

Conforms to contracts/EVENT_CONTRACT.md.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timezone
from enum import Enum
import hashlib
from typing import Any, Optional
from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, model_validator


class TriggerType(str, Enum):
    ALERT = "alert"
    MANUAL = "manual"


class AlertDetails(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="ignore")

    name: str = Field(..., description="Alert rule name e.g. MachineConfigControllerDrainError")
    fingerprint: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Stable alert fingerprint from Alertmanager")
    severity: str = Field("warning", description="Alert severity level (warning, critical, info)")
    labels: dict[str, str] = Field(default_factory=dict, description="Raw alert labels from Alertmanager")
    annotations: dict[str, str] = Field(default_factory=dict, description="Alert annotations / descriptions")


class RequestDetails(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="ignore")

    text: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="User or ticket investigation brief (untrusted text)")
    requested_by: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Authenticated principal requesting investigation")
    change_id: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Associated change request / CRQ ID")


class EventEnvelope(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    schema_version: str = Field("1.0", description="Contract schema version")
    event_id: str = Field(..., description="Unique event identifier")
    event_type: str = Field(..., description="Reverse-DNS event type e.g. com.example.openshift.mco.drain.alert")
    occurred_at: datetime = Field(..., description="Timestamp when the event occurred at source")
    received_at: datetime = Field(..., description="Timestamp when the event entered the pipeline")
    trigger_type: TriggerType = Field(..., description="Ingress trigger mode: alert or manual")
    source: str = Field(..., description="Ingress source e.g. alertmanager, web-console, cli")
    cluster_id: str = Field(..., min_length=1, description="Explicit target cluster ID")
    cluster_name: str = Field(..., min_length=1, description="Cluster human-readable name")
    openshift_version: str = Field(..., description="OpenShift release version e.g. 4.22.z")
    node_name: str = Field(..., min_length=1, description="Target node undergoing drain / maintenance")
    mcp_name: str = Field(..., min_length=1, description="Target MachineConfigPool name (e.g. worker, master)")
    alert: Optional[AlertDetails] = Field(None, description="Alert payload when trigger_type=alert")
    request: Optional[RequestDetails] = Field(None, description="User request payload when trigger_type=manual")
    trace_id: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Distributed trace ID for observability correlation")

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_trigger_consistency(self) -> EventEnvelope:
        if self.schema_version != "1.0":
            raise ValueError(f"Unsupported schema_version: {self.schema_version}. Expected '1.0'")
        
        if self.trigger_type == TriggerType.ALERT:
            if not self.alert or not self.alert.name:
                raise ValueError("trigger_type='alert' requires an 'alert' payload with a valid 'name'")
        elif self.trigger_type == TriggerType.MANUAL:
            if not self.request or not self.request.requested_by:
                raise ValueError("trigger_type='manual' requires an authenticated principal in 'request.requested_by'")
        return self

    def generate_idempotency_key(self, bucket_minutes: int = 15) -> str:
        """Computes a stable idempotency key bucketed by time.
        
        Preferred formula from contracts/EVENT_CONTRACT.md:
        fingerprint/event_id + cluster_id + alert_name/manual + node_name + time_bucket
        """
        # Truncate occurred_at to time bucket
        ts = int(self.occurred_at.timestamp())
        bucket_seconds = bucket_minutes * 60
        bucket_id = ts // bucket_seconds

        sig_source = self.alert.fingerprint if (self.alert and self.alert.fingerprint) else self.event_id
        signal_name = self.alert.name if self.alert else "manual_investigation"

        raw = f"{sig_source}:{self.cluster_id}:{signal_name}:{self.node_name}:{bucket_id}"
        return hashlib.sha256(raw.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()

=== END FILE ===

=== FILE: schemas/evidence.py ===
"""Authoritative Evidence Record Schema.

Conforms to contracts/EVIDENCE_SCHEMA.md.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime
from enum import Enum
from typing import Any, Optional
from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field


class EvidenceType(str, Enum):
    LOG = "log"
    OBJECT_SNAPSHOT = "object_snapshot"
    OBJECT_CHANGE = "object_change"
    EVENT = "event"
    METRIC = "metric"
    AUDIT = "audit"


class Freshness(str, Enum):
    FRESH = "fresh"
    STALE = "stale"
    UNKNOWN = "unknown"


class Quality(str, Enum):
    VALID = "valid"
    PARTIAL = "partial"
    MALFORMED = "malformed"
    CONTRADICTORY = "contradictory"


class RedactionStatus(str, Enum):
    REDACTED = "redacted"
    UNREDACTED = "unredacted"
    NONE_NEEDED = "none_needed"


class ResourceIdentifier(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="ignore")

    api_version: str = Field(..., description="Kubernetes apiVersion e.g. policy/v1, v1")
    kind: str = Field(..., description="Kubernetes Kind e.g. PodDisruptionBudget, Pod, Node")
    namespace: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Namespace if resource is namespaced")
    name: str = Field(..., description="Resource name")
    uid: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Kubernetes object UID")
    resource_version: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Resource version for ordering snapshots")


class MetricPayload(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="ignore")

    metric_name: str = Field(..., description="Prometheus metric name")
    labels: dict[str, str] = Field(default_factory=dict, description="Metric label dimensions")
    value: float = Field(..., description="Evaluated metric float value")
    unit: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Metric unit e.g. bytes, ratio, seconds")
    sample_timestamp: datetime = Field(..., description="Timestamp of the Prometheus sample")
    aggregation_window: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Aggregation window e.g. 5m, 1h")
    query_resolution: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="PromQL step resolution e.g. 15s")


class EvidenceRecord(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="ignore")

    evidence_id: str = Field(..., description="Unique evidence ID (ev-*)")
    source_adapter: str = Field(..., description="Adapter name: splunk_mcp or prometheus_mcp")
    source_dataset: str = Field(..., description="Dataset name e.g. ocp_logs, k8s_objects, ocp_metrics")
    source_query_id: str = Field(..., description="Traceable query execution ID")
    cluster_id: str = Field(..., description="Cluster from which telemetry was extracted")
    resource: Optional[ResourceIdentifier] = Field(None, description="Kubernetes resource target")
    evidence_type: EvidenceType = Field(..., description="Type of evidence record")
    event_time: datetime = Field(..., description="When the raw event occurred in the cluster")
    observed_at: datetime = Field(..., description="When telemetry was observed by monitoring")
    ingested_at: datetime = Field(..., description="When record was ingested into telemetry backend")
    retrieved_at: datetime = Field(..., description="When adapter executed the query and fetched it")
    freshness: Freshness = Field(Freshness.FRESH, description="Freshness evaluation against matrix limits")
    quality: Quality = Field(Quality.VALID, description="Quality classification of evidence")
    fields: dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict, description="Normalized key-value attributes")
    excerpt: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=2000, description="Bounded redacted excerpt of log/object")
    content_hash: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="SHA256 hash of raw extracted payload")
    redaction_status: RedactionStatus = Field(RedactionStatus.REDACTED, description="Redaction gate status")
    collector_version: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Telemetry collector version")
    source_revision: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Telemetry source revision")
    metric: Optional[MetricPayload] = Field(None, description="Typed metric details when evidence_type=metric")

=== END FILE ===

=== FILE: schemas/report.py ===
"""Authoritative Investigation Report and Remediation Output Schemas.

Conforms to contracts/TOOL_CONTRACTS.md §4, contracts/EVIDENCE_SCHEMA.md §4,
and eval fixtures.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime
from enum import Enum
from typing import Any, Optional, Union
from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, model_validator


class Classification(str, Enum):
    SUPPORTED = "supported"
    NOT_SUPPORTED = "not_supported"
    INCONCLUSIVE = "inconclusive"
    PROBABLE = "probable"


class Confidence(str, Enum):
    HIGH = "high"
    MEDIUM = "medium"
    LOW = "low"


class RemediationType(str, Enum):
    ANSIBLE_PLAYBOOK = "ANSIBLE_PLAYBOOK"
    ANSIBLE_PLAYBOOK_PSEUDOCODE = "ANSIBLE_PLAYBOOK_PSEUDOCODE"
    MANUAL_CLI_RUNBOOK = "MANUAL_CLI_RUNBOOK"
    NONE = "none"


class RemediationKind(str, Enum):
    PLAYBOOK = "playbook"
    MANUAL_CLI = "manual_cli"
    NONE = "none"


class TargetContext(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="ignore")

    cluster_id: str = Field(..., description="Target cluster ID")
    target_namespace: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Namespace of affected workload")
    failing_pod: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Name of pod stalling eviction")
    target_node: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Node being drained")
    target_pdb: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="PodDisruptionBudget name if applicable")


class EvidenceCitation(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="ignore")

    source: str = Field(..., description="Source dataset e.g. ocp_logs, k8s_objects, ocp_metrics")
    excerpt: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Log line or description excerpt")
    resource: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Kind/name of resource e.g. PodDisruptionBudget/payments-api-pdb")
    field: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Field path e.g. status.disruptionsAllowed")
    value: Optional[Any] = Field(None, description="Observed value of the field")


class RootCause(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="ignore")

    summary: str = Field(..., description="Clear explanatory synthesis of the root cause")
    sub_branch: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Decision tree branch e.g. 3B_multi_replica_at_min_available")
    evidence_citations: list[EvidenceCitation] = Field(default_factory=list, description="Citations to verified evidence")


class ManualCLIFallback(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="ignore")

    command: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Recommended safe manual inspection or scale command")
    verification: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Command to verify recovery")
    risk_level: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Risk assessment: low, medium, high")
    pre_checks: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list, description="Pre-execution sanity check commands")
    remediation_steps: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list, description="Step-by-step CLI commands")
    safety_warning: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Explicit warning on what NOT to do")


class RemediationReport(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="ignore")

    remediation_type: RemediationType = Field(RemediationType.NONE, description="Remediation approach type")
    playbook_match: bool = Field(False, description="Whether root cause cleanly matched an approved playbook")
    playbook_id: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Playbook ID in PLAYBOOK_CATALOG.json")
    playbook_file: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Relative path to playbook YAML")
    risk_level: Optional[str] = Field("low", description="Operational risk level of remediation")
    requires_human_approval: bool = Field(True, description="Strict requirement for human SRE gate")
    ansible_pseudocode: Optional[Union[list[str], str]] = Field(None, description="Day-1 Ansible YAML task spec")
    manual_cli_fallback: Optional[Union[ManualCLIFallback, dict[str, Any]]] = Field(None, description="Guarded CLI alternative")
    aap_launch_spec: Optional[dict[str, Any]] = Field(None, description="Ansible Automation Platform extra vars")
    verification_goal: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Target post-remediation condition")


class Hypothesis(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="ignore")

    hypothesis_id: str = Field(..., description="Unique hypothesis identifier")
    title: str = Field(..., description="Hypothesis description")
    status: str = Field(..., description="supported, contradicted, unresolved")
    confidence: Confidence = Field(..., description="high, medium, low")
    rationale: str = Field(..., description="Justification grounded in evidence")
    supporting_evidence_ids: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list, description="Cited supporting evidence IDs")
    contradicting_evidence_ids: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list, description="Cited contradicting evidence IDs")


class InvestigationReport(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="ignore")

    schema_version: str = Field("1.0", description="Report contract schema version")
    investigation_id: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Investigation run UUID")
    investigation_status: str = Field("completed", description="Lifecycle outcome of investigation")
    classification: Classification = Field(..., description="Overall diagnostic classification")
    confidence: Confidence = Field(..., description="Confidence level: high, medium, low")
    lifecycle_stage: Optional[str] = Field("drain", description="Evaluated node lifecycle stage")
    use_case_id: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Diagnosed use case ID e.g. 01-pdb-eviction-blocked")
    stop_reason: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Canonical stop reason code from contracts/REASON_CODES.md")
    target_context: Optional[TargetContext] = Field(None, description="Cluster, node, workload context")
    root_cause: Optional[RootCause] = Field(None, description="Root cause synthesis and evidence citations")
    remediation: RemediationReport = Field(default_factory=RemediationReport, description="Structured remediation output")
    hypotheses: list[Hypothesis] = Field(default_factory=list, description="Evaluated causal hypotheses")
    execution_timing_ms: dict[str, int] = Field(default_factory=dict, description="Performance latency timings")
    created_at: Optional[datetime] = Field(None, description="Report generation timestamp")

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_safety_and_remediation_policy(self) -> InvestigationReport:
        # Non-negotiable policy: No remediation allowed on inconclusive or not_supported
        if self.classification in (Classification.INCONCLUSIVE, Classification.NOT_SUPPORTED):
            if self.remediation.playbook_match:
                raise ValueError(f"playbook_match cannot be True when classification is {self.classification.value}")
            if self.remediation.playbook_id is not None:
                raise ValueError(f"playbook_id must be None when classification is {self.classification.value}")
        
        # Supported/high must not carry an inconclusive stop reason
        if self.classification == Classification.SUPPORTED and self.confidence == Confidence.HIGH:
            if self.stop_reason is not None:
                raise ValueError("supported/high finding must not carry a stop_reason")
        
        return self


class OracleOutput(BaseModel):
    """Simplified evaluation shape matching uc01_reference oracle."""
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="ignore")

    classification: Classification
    confidence: Confidence
    sub_branch: Optional[str] = None
    stop_reason: Optional[str] = None
    remediation_kind: RemediationKind = RemediationKind.NONE
    playbook_id: Optional[str] = None
    extra_vars: Optional[dict[str, Any]] = None

=== END FILE ===

=== FILE: schemas/state.py ===
"""Authoritative Agent State Schema for LangGraph.

Conforms to DEVELOPMENT_BLUEPRINT.md §4 and architecture/LANGGRAPH_DESIGN.md.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime
from enum import Enum
from typing import Any, Optional, TypedDict
from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field
from .dossier import Dossier


class TrackType(str, Enum):
    TRACK_A = "track_a"  # Known deterministic skills (01-16)
    TRACK_B = "track_b"  # Exploratory unknown anomaly


class LifecycleStage(str, Enum):
    DRAIN = "drain"
    CONFIG_APPLY = "config_apply"
    PIVOT_REBOOT = "pivot_reboot"
    UNCORDON = "uncordon"
    MCP_ROLLOUT = "mcp_rollout"
    CLUSTER_UPGRADE = "cluster_upgrade"
    UNKNOWN = "unknown"


class PersistStatus(str, Enum):
    PENDING = "pending"
    PERSISTED = "persisted"
    FAILED = "failed"


class AgentState(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="ignore")

    # Identifiers & Ingress Context
    investigation_id: str = Field(..., description="Unique investigation run ID")
    idempotency_key: str = Field(..., description="Deduplication key for ingress event")
    event_id: str = Field(..., description="Source event ID")
    trigger_type: str = Field("alert", description="alert or manual")

    # Normalized Cluster Context
    cluster_id: str = Field(..., description="Target OpenShift cluster ID")
    cluster_name: str = Field(..., description="Cluster human-readable name")
    openshift_version: str = Field(..., description="OpenShift release version")
    mco_version: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="MCO operator version")
    node_name: str = Field(..., description="Target node name")
    mcp_name: str = Field(..., description="MachineConfigPool name")

    # Lifecycle & Workflow Routing
    lifecycle_stage: LifecycleStage = Field(LifecycleStage.UNKNOWN, description="Classified lifecycle stage")
    use_case_id: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Matched use case ID e.g. 01-pdb-eviction-blocked")
    skill_version: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Loaded skill semver version")
    track: TrackType = Field(TrackType.TRACK_A, description="Selected execution track: track_a or track_b")

    # Runtime Budgets & Deadlines
    started_at: datetime = Field(..., description="Investigation start timestamp")
    deadline_at: datetime = Field(..., description="Hard 45s execution deadline")
    query_count_by_adapter: dict[str, int] = Field(
        default_factory=lambda: {"splunk_mcp": 0, "prometheus_mcp": 0},
        description="Adapter query call counts"
    )
    retry_count_by_adapter: dict[str, int] = Field(
        default_factory=lambda: {"splunk_mcp": 0, "prometheus_mcp": 0},
        description="Adapter retry counts"
    )

    # Evidence & Correlation State
    dossier: Optional[Dossier] = Field(None, description="Normalized evidence dossier")
    evidence_refs: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list, description="List of evidence IDs retained in context")
    evidence_quality: str = Field("valid", description="Aggregate evidence quality marker")
    missing_evidence: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list, description="Missing mandatory evidence keys")
    contradictions: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list, description="Detected telemetry contradictions")

    # Diagnostic Findings
    classification: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="supported, not_supported, inconclusive, probable")
    confidence: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="high, medium, low")
    sub_branch: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Sub-branch identified in skill decision tree")
    hypotheses: list[dict[str, Any]] = Field(default_factory=list, description="Ranked causal hypotheses")
    supporting_evidence_ids: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list, description="Evidence IDs proving the hypothesis")
    contradicting_evidence_ids: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list, description="Evidence IDs conflicting with hypothesis")

    # Remediation
    recommendation_type: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="ANSIBLE_PLAYBOOK_PSEUDOCODE, MANUAL_CLI_RUNBOOK, NONE")
    playbook_id: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Catalog playbook ID if matched")
    catalog_revision: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Remediation catalog revision")
    recommendation_payload: Optional[dict[str, Any]] = Field(None, description="Full remediation output spec")
    extra_vars: Optional[dict[str, Any]] = Field(None, description="AAP launch extra vars if playbook matched")

    # Persistence & Termination
    persist_status: PersistStatus = Field(PersistStatus.PENDING, description="PostgreSQL persistence state")
    terminal_reason: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Canonical reason code from contracts/REASON_CODES.md")
    error: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Unrecoverable runtime error if failed")


class AgentStateDict(TypedDict, total=False):
    """TypedDict representation for LangGraph node signatures."""
    investigation_id: str
    idempotency_key: str
    event_id: str
    trigger_type: str
    cluster_id: str
    cluster_name: str
    openshift_version: str
    mco_version: Optional[str]
    node_name: str
    mcp_name: str
    lifecycle_stage: str
    use_case_id: Optional[str]
    skill_version: Optional[str]
    track: str
    started_at: datetime
    deadline_at: datetime
    query_count_by_adapter: dict[str, int]
    retry_count_by_adapter: dict[str, int]
    dossier: Optional[dict[str, Any]]
    evidence_refs: list[str]
    evidence_quality: str
    missing_evidence: list[str]
    contradictions: list[str]
    classification: Optional[str]
    confidence: Optional[str]
    sub_branch: Optional[str]
    hypotheses: list[dict[str, Any]]
    supporting_evidence_ids: list[str]
    contradicting_evidence_ids: list[str]
    recommendation_type: Optional[str]
    playbook_id: Optional[str]
    catalog_revision: Optional[str]
    recommendation_payload: Optional[dict[str, Any]]
    extra_vars: Optional[dict[str, Any]]
    persist_status: str
    terminal_reason: Optional[str]
    error: Optional[str]

=== END FILE ===
